  │ 482 +     /**                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 483 +      * Runs benchmark with simulated GC pauses.                                                                                                  │
  │ 484 +      */                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 485 +     private static long runBenchmarkWithGCPauses(int queueDepth, long taskBudgetNs,                                                              │
  │ 486 +                                                    TimingProfile profile, int pauseMs, int pauseFreq) {                                          │
  │ 487 +         currentTimingProfile = profile;                                                                                                          │
  │ 488 +         ConcurrentLinkedQueue<SimulatedChunkEvent> chunkQueue = new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>();                                                   │
  │ 489 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 490 +         for (int i = 0; i < queueDepth; i++) {                                                                                                   │
  │ 491 +             chunkQueue.offer(new SimulatedChunkEvent(i % 1000 - 500, i / 1000 - 500));                                                           │
  │ 492 +         }                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 493 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 494 +         long start = System.nanoTime();                                                                                                          │
  │ 495 +         int processed = 0;                                                                                                                       │
  │ 496 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 497 +         while (!chunkQueue.isEmpty()) {                                                                                                          │
  │ 498 +             SimulatedChunkEvent event = chunkQueue.poll();                                                                                       │
  │ 499 +             if (event == null) break;                                                                                                            │
  │ 500 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 501 +             processChunkEvent(event);                                                                                                            │
  │ 502 +             processed++;                                                                                                                         │
  │ 503 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 504 +             // Simulate GC pause at specified frequency                                                                                          │
  │ 505 +             if (pauseMs > 0 && pauseFreq > 0 && processed % pauseFreq == 0) {                                                                    │
  │ 506 +                 try {                                                                                                                            │
  │ 507 +                     Thread.sleep(pauseMs);                                                                                                       │
  │ 508 +                 } catch (InterruptedException e) {                                                                                               │
  │ 509 +                     Thread.currentThread().interrupt();                                                                                          │
  │ 510 +                 }                                                                                                                                │
  │ 511 +             }                                                                                                                                    │
  │ 512 +         }                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 513 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 514 +         return System.nanoTime() - start;                                                                                                        │
  │ 515 +     }                                                                                                                                            │

  │ 550 +     private static class ConcurrentProducerResult {                                                                                              │
  │ 551 +         final int drained;                                                                                                                       │
  │ 552 +         final int remaining;                                                                                                                     │
  │ 553 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 554 +         ConcurrentProducerResult(int drained, int remaining) {                                                                                   │
  │ 555 +             this.drained = drained;                                                                                                              │
  │ 556 +             this.remaining = remaining;                                                                                                          │
  │ 557 +         }                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 558 +     }                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 559 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 560 +     private static ConcurrentProducerResult runConcurrentProducerBenchmark(                                                                      │
  │ 561 +             int initialDepth, long taskBudgetNs, TimingProfile profile, int addRate) {                                                           │
  │ 562 +         currentTimingProfile = profile;                                                                                                          │
  │ 563 +         ConcurrentLinkedQueue<SimulatedChunkEvent> chunkQueue = new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>();                                                   │
  │ 564 +         AtomicLong eventsAdded = new AtomicLong(0);                                                                                              │
  │ 565 +         AtomicLong eventsDrained = new AtomicLong(0);                                                                                            │
  │ 566 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 567 +         // Populate initial queue                                                                                                                │
  │ 568 +         for (int i = 0; i < initialDepth; i++) {                                                                                                 │
  │ 569 +             chunkQueue.offer(new SimulatedChunkEvent(i % 1000 - 500, i / 1000 - 500));                                                           │
  │ 570 +         }                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 571 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 572 +         // Producer thread                                                                                                                       │
  │ 573 +         Thread producer = new Thread(() -> {                                                                                                     │
  │ 574 +             int added = 0;                                                                                                                       │
  │ 575 +             while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {                                                                                    │
  │ 576 +                 for (int i = 0; i < addRate; i++) {                                                                                              │
  │ 577 +                     chunkQueue.offer(new SimulatedChunkEvent(added % 1000 - 500, added / 1000));                                                 │
  │ 578 +                     added++;                                                                                                                     │
  │ 579 +                 }                                                                                                                                │
  │ 580 +                 eventsAdded.set(added);                                                                                                          │
  │ 581 +                 try {                                                                                                                            │
  │ 582 +                     Thread.sleep(50); // Add events every 50ms                                                                                   │
  │ 583 +                 } catch (InterruptedException e) {                                                                                               │
  │ 584 +                     break;                                                                                                                       │
  │ 585 +                 }                                                                                                                                │
  │ 586 +             }                                                                                                                                    │
  │ 587 +         });                                                                                                                                      │
  │ 588 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 589 +         producer.start();                                                                                                                        │
  │ 590 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 591 +         // Consumer (tick handler) - process for 100ms (2 ticks at 20 TPS)                                                                       │
  │ 592 +         long deadline = System.nanoTime() + TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(100);                                                                  │
  │ 593 +         int drained = 0;                                                                                                                         │
  │ 594 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 595 +         while (System.nanoTime() < deadline && !chunkQueue.isEmpty()) {                                                                          │
  │ 596 +             SimulatedChunkEvent event = chunkQueue.poll();                                                                                       │
  │ 597 +             if (event == null) continue;                                                                                                         │
  │ 598 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 599 +             processChunkEvent(event);                                                                                                            │
  │ 600 +             drained++;                                                                                                                           │
  │ 601 +         }                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 602 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 603 +         eventsDrained.set(drained);                                                                                                              │
  │ 604 +         producer.interrupt();                                                                                                                    │
  │ 605 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 606 +         return new ConcurrentProducerResult(drained, chunkQueue.size());                                                                         │
  │ 607 +     }                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 608 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │  23 +     ADDITION = auto()                                                                                                                            │
  │  24 +     MULTIPLICATION = auto()                                                                                                                      │

  │  46 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  47 +     Axioms:                                                                                                                                      │
  │  48 +     1. Closure: ∀a,b ∈ G, a*b ∈ G                                                                                                                │
  │  49 +     2. Associativity: ∀a,b,c ∈ G, (a*b)*c = a*(b*c)                                                                                              │
  │  50 +     3. Identity: ∃e ∈ G, ∀a ∈ G, e*a = a*e = a                                                                                                   │
  │  51 +     4. Inverse: ∀a ∈ G, ∃a⁻¹ ∈ G, a*a⁻¹ = a⁻¹*a = e                                                                                              │

  │  53 +     name: str                                                                                                                                    │

  │  70 +     def op(self, a: GroupElement, b: GroupElement) -> Tuple[GroupElement, ProofObject]:                                                          │
  │  71 +         """Group operation with proof."""                                                                                                        │
  │  72 +         if a.group != self or b.group != self:                                                                                                   │
  │  73 +             raise ValueError("Elements must belong to this group")                                                                               │
  │  74 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  75 +         result_value = self.operation(a.value, b.value)                                                                                          │
  │  76 +         result = self.create_element(result_value)                                                                                               │
  │  77 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  78 +         # CLOSURE AXIOM: result must be in group                                                                                                 │
  │  79 +         closure_claim = YeshuaClaim(                                                                                                             │
  │  80 +             claim_id=f"closure_{a.value}_{b.value}",                                                                                             │
  │  81 +             statement=f"{result_value} ∈ {self.name}",                                                                                           │
  │  82 +             axiom_basis=[YeshuaAxiom.AXIOM_3],  # No hidden state                                                                                │
  │  83 +             derivation=["closure_axiom"]                                                                                                         │
  │  84 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │  85 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  86 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │  87 +             conclusion=f"op({a.value}, {b.value}) = {result_value}",                                                                             │
  │  88 +             premises=[f"{a.value} ∈ {self.name}", f"{b.value} ∈ {self.name}"],                                                                   │
  │  89 +             rule="closure",                                                                                                                      │
  │  90 +             derivation=[closure_claim]                                                                                                           │
  │  91 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │  92 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  93 +         return result, proof                                                                                                                     │
  │  94 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  95 +     def check_associativity(self) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                                   │
  │  96 +         """Verify associativity: (a*b)*c = a*(b*c) for all a,b,c."""                                                                             │
  │  97 +         elements_list = list(self.elements)                                                                                                      │
  │  98 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  99 +         for a in elements_list:                                                                                                                  │
  │ 100 +             for b in elements_list:                                                                                                              │
  │ 101 +                 for c in elements_list:                                                                                                          │
  │ 102 +                     left = self.operation(self.operation(a.value, b.value), c.value)                                                             │
  │ 103 +                     right = self.operation(a.value, self.operation(b.value, c.value))                                                            │
  │ 104 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 105 +                     if not self.element_eq(left, right):                                                                                         │

  │ 108 +                             premises=[f"a={a.value}, b={b.value}, c={c.value}"],                                                                 │
  │ 109 +                             rule="counterexample",                                                                                               │
  │ 110 +                             derivation=[]                                                                                                        │
  │ 111 +                         )                                                                                                                        │
  │ 112 +                         return False, proof                                                                                                      │
  │ 113 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 114 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 115 +             conclusion=f"{self.name} is associative",                                                                                            │
  │ 116 +             premises=[f"checked {len(elements_list)}³ triples"],                                                                                 │
  │ 117 +             rule="exhaustive_verification",                                                                                                      │
  │ 118 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 119 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 120 +         return True, proof                                                                                                                       │
  │ 121 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 122 +     def check_identity(self, candidate: Any) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                        │
  │ 123 +         """Verify e is the identity element."""                                                                                                  │
  │ 124 +         for a in self.elements:                                                                                                                  │
  │ 125 +             left = self.operation(candidate, a.value)                                                                                            │
  │ 126 +             right = self.operation(a.value, candidate)                                                                                           │
  │ 127 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 128 +             if not (self.element_eq(left, a.value) and self.element_eq(right, a.value)):                                                         │

  │ 130 +                     conclusion=f"{candidate} is not identity",                                                                                   │
  │ 131 +                     premises=[f"failed for a={a.value}"],                                                                                        │
  │ 132 +                     rule="counterexample",                                                                                                       │
  │ 133 +                     derivation=[]                                                                                                                │
  │ 134 +                 )                                                                                                                                │
  │ 135 +                 return False, proof                                                                                                              │
  │ 136 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 137 +         self.identity_value = candidate                                                                                                          │
  │ 138 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 139 +             conclusion=f"{candidate} is identity of {self.name}",                                                                                │

  │ 141 +             rule="universal_verification",                                                                                                       │
  │ 142 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 143 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 144 +         return True, proof                                                                                                                       │
  │ 145 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 146 +     def check_inverse(self, a: GroupElement, candidate: Any) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                        │
  │ 147 +         """Verify candidate is the inverse of a."""                                                                                              │
  │ 148 +         prod1 = self.operation(a.value, candidate)                                                                                               │

  │ 193 + class CyclicGroup(Group):                                                                                                                        │
  │ 194 +     """Cyclic group Z_n (additive) or C_n (multiplicative)."""                                                                                   │

  │ 219 +             identity_value=identity                                                                                                              │

  │ 227 +         """Order of element a (smallest k such that a^k = e)."""                                                                                 │
  │ 228 +         current = self.identity_value                                                                                                            │

  │ 230 +             current = self.operation(current, a.value)                                                                                           │
  │ 231 +             if current == self.identity_value:                                                                                                   │

  │ 233 +         return self.n                                                                                                                            │

  │ 254 +         proofs = []                                                                                                                              │

  │ 269 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │ 271 +         for a in self.elements:                                                                                                                  │
  │ 272 +             for b in self.elements:                                                                                                              │
  │ 273 +                 for c in self.elements:                                                                                                          │
  │ 274 +                     # Left distributivity                                                                                                        │
  │ 275 +                     left1 = self.mul(a, self.add(b, c))                                                                                          │
  │ 276 +                     right1 = self.add(self.mul(a, b), self.mul(a, c))                                                                            │
  │ 277 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │ 279 +                     left2 = self.mul(self.add(a, b), c)                                                                                          │
  │ 280 +                     right2 = self.add(self.mul(a, c), self.mul(b, c))                                                                            │

  │ 286 +                             rule="counterexample",                                                                                               │

  │ 288 +                         ))                                                                                                                       │

  │ 295 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 296 +         ))                                                                                                                                       │

  │ 302 + class Ideal:                                                                                                                                     │
  │ 303 +     """An ideal I of a ring R.                                                                                                                   │
  │ 304 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 305 +     Properties:                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 306 +     1. (I, +) is a subgroup of (R, +)                                                                                                            │
  │ 307 +     2. ∀r ∈ R, ∀i ∈ I: r*i ∈ I and i*r ∈ I (absorption)                                                                                          │

  │ 315 +         # Check subgroup under addition                                                                                                          │

  │ 323 +                         derivation=[]                                                                                                            │

  │ 325 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 326 +         # Check absorption                                                                                                                       │
  │ 327 +         for r in self.ring.elements:                                                                                                             │
  │ 328 +             for i in self.elements:                                                                                                              │
  │ 329 +                 if self.ring.mul(r, i) not in self.elements:                                                                                     │
  │ 330 +                     return False, ProofObject(                                                                                                   │
  │ 331 +                         conclusion="Absorption failed",                                                                                          │

  │ 333 +                         rule="absorption_failure",                                                                                               │
  │ 334 +                         derivation=[]                                                                                                            │
  │ 335 +                     )                                                                                                                            │
  │ 336 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 337 +         return True, ProofObject(                                                                                                                │
  │ 338 +             conclusion=f"{self.name} is an ideal of {self.ring.name}",                                                                           │
  │ 339 +             premises=["closed under addition", "absorbs multiplication"],                                                                        │
  │ 340 +             rule="ideal_definition",                                                                                                             │

  │ 349 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │ 351 + def check_cauchy_theorem(group: Group, p: int) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[GroupElement], ProofObject]:                                              │
  │ 352 +     """Cauchy's Theorem: if p | |G|, then G has an element of order p."""                                                                        │
  │ 353 +     order = len(group.elements)                                                                                                                  │
  │ 354 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 355 +     if order % p != 0:                                                                                                                           │
  │ 356 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 357 +             conclusion=f"Cauchy not applicable: p={p} does not divide |G|={order}",                                                              │
  │ 358 +             premises=[],                                                                                                                         │
  │ 359 +             rule="cauchy_precondition",                                                                                                          │
  │ 360 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │

  │ 362 +         return False, None, proof                                                                                                                │

  │ 366 +         if group.order_of_element(elem) == p:                                                                                                    │
  │ 367 +             proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                 │

  │ 369 +                 premises=[f"order({elem.value}) = {p}"],                                                                                         │
  │ 370 +                 rule="cauchy_theorem",                                                                                                           │
  │ 371 +                 derivation=[]                                                                                                                    │
  │ 372 +             )                                                                                                                                    │
  │ 373 +             return True, elem, proof                                                                                                             │

  │ 377 +         premises=[],                                                                                                                             │
  │ 378 +         rule="search_failure",                                                                                                                   │
  │ 379 +         derivation=[]                                                                                                                            │
  │ 380 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 381 +     return False, None, proof                                                                                                                    │

  │  40 +     def __post_init__(self):                                                                                                                     │
  │  41 +         """Validate topology axioms."""                                                                                                          │
  │  42 +         # Verify ∅ and X are open                                                                                                                │
  │  43 +         empty = frozenset()                                                                                                                      │
  │  44 +         whole = frozenset(self.underlying_set)                                                                                                   │
  │  45 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  46 +         assert empty in self.open_sets, "Empty set must be open"                                                                                 │
  │  47 +         assert whole in self.open_sets, "Whole space must be open"                                                                               │

  │  49 +     def is_open(self, subset: Set[Point]) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                           │
  │  50 +         """Check if subset is open."""                                                                                                           │
  │  51 +         subset_frozen = frozenset(subset)                                                                                                        │
  │  52 +         is_open = subset_frozen in self.open_sets                                                                                                │
  │  53 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │  63 +         """Check if subset is closed (complement is open)."""                                                                                    │
  │  64 +         complement = self.underlying_set - subset                                                                                                │
  │  65 +         complement_frozen = frozenset(complement)                                                                                                │
  │  66 +         is_closed = complement_frozen in self.open_sets                                                                                          │
  │  67 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  68 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │  69 +             conclusion=f"{subset} is {'closed' if is_closed else 'not closed'}",                                                                 │
  │  70 +             premises=[f"complement {complement} is {'open' if complement_frozen in self.open_sets else 'not open'}"],                            │
  │  71 +             rule="closed_definition",                                                                                                            │
  │  72 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │  73 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │  74 +         return is_closed, proof                                                                                                                  │
  │  75 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  76 +     def closure(self, subset: Set[Point]) -> Tuple[Set[Point], ProofObject]:                                                                     │
  │  77 +         """Closure of a set: smallest closed set containing subset."""                                                                           │
  │  78 +         # Closure = subset ∪ limit points                                                                                                        │
  │  79 +         closure_set = set(subset)                                                                                                                │
  │  80 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  81 +         for p in self.underlying_set:                                                                                                            │
  │  82 +             if p in subset:                                                                                                                      │
  │  83 +                 continue                                                                                                                         │
  │  84 +             # p is a limit point if every open set containing p intersects subset                                                                │
  │  85 +             is_limit_point = True                                                                                                                │
  │  86 +             for open_set in self.open_sets:                                                                                                      │
  │  87 +                 if p in open_set:                                                                                                                │
  │  88 +                     if not (open_set & subset):                                                                                                  │
  │  89 +                         is_limit_point = False                                                                                                   │
  │  90 +                         break                                                                                                                    │
  │  91 +             if is_limit_point:                                                                                                                   │
  │  92 +                 closure_set.add(p)                                                                                                               │
  │  93 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  94 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │  95 +             conclusion=f"closure({subset}) = {closure_set}",                                                                                     │
  │  96 +             premises=["limit point analysis"],                                                                                                   │
  │  97 +             rule="closure_definition",                                                                                                           │
  │  98 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │  99 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 100 +         return closure_set, proof                                                                                                                │
  │ 101 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 102 +     def interior(self, subset: Set[Point]) -> Tuple[Set[Point], ProofObject]:                                                                    │
  │ 103 +         """Interior of a set: largest open set contained in subset."""                                                                           │
  │ 104 +         # Interior = union of all open sets contained in subset                                                                                  │
  │ 105 +         interior_set = set()                                                                                                                     │
  │ 106 +         subset_frozen = frozenset(subset)                                                                                                        │
  │ 107 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 108 +         for open_set in self.open_sets:                                                                                                          │

  │ 110 +                 interior_set |= open_set                                                                                                         │
  │ 111 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │ 113 +             conclusion=f"interior({subset}) = {interior_set}",                                                                                   │

  │ 116 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 117 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 118 +         return interior_set, proof                                                                                                               │
  │ 119 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 120 +     def boundary(self, subset: Set[Point]) -> Tuple[Set[Point], ProofObject]:                                                                    │
  │ 121 +         """Boundary of a set: closure(A) ∩ closure(X\A)."""                                                                                      │
  │ 122 +         closure_a, _ = self.closure(subset)                                                                                                      │

  │ 132 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 133 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 134 +         return boundary_set, proof                                                                                                               │
  │ 135 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 136 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 137 + def check_hausdorff(space: TopologicalSpace) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                        │
  │ 138 +     """Verify Hausdorff (T2) property: distinct points have disjoint neighborhoods.                                                              │
  │ 139 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 140 +     ∀x,y ∈ X, x≠y ⇒ ∃U,V open: x∈U, y∈V, U∩V=∅                                                                                                   │

  │ 162 +                 )                                                                                                                                │
  │ 163 +                 return False, proof                                                                                                              │
  │ 164 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │ 174 + def check_compact(space: TopologicalSpace, cover: List[Set[Point]]) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[List[Set[Point]]], ProofObject]:                     │
  │ 175 +     """Verify compactness: every open cover has a finite subcover.                                                                               │

  │ 188 +             rule="cover_invalid",                                                                                                                │
  │ 189 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 190 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 191 +         return False, None, proof                                                                                                                │
  │ 192 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 193 +     # Try to find finite subcover (exponential search - only practical for small spaces)                                                         │
  │ 194 +     from itertools import combinations                                                                                                           │
  │ 195 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │ 202 +                 proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                             │
  │ 203 +                     conclusion=f"Found finite subcover of size {r}",                                                                             │
  │ 204 +                     premises=[f"subcover covers {space.name}"],                                                                                  │
  │ 205 +                     rule="compact_verification",                                                                                                 │
  │ 206 +                     derivation=[]                                                                                                                │
  │ 207 +                 )                                                                                                                                │
  │ 208 +                 return True, list(subcover), proof                                                                                               │
  │ 209 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 210 +     proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                         │
  │ 211 +         conclusion=f"No finite subcover found",                                                                                                  │
  │ 212 +         premises=[f"checked all subsets of cover"],                                                                                              │
  │ 213 +         rule="compact_failure",                                                                                                                  │
  │ 214 +         derivation=[]                                                                                                                            │
  │ 215 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 216 +     return False, None, proof                                                                                                                    │

  │ 218 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 219 + def check_connected(space: TopologicalSpace) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                        │
  │ 220 +     """Verify connectedness: cannot be partitioned into two disjoint non-empty open sets.                                                        │
  │ 221 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 222 +     X is connected if it cannot be written as U ∪ V where U,V are disjoint non-empty open sets.                                                  │
  │ 223 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 224 +     # Check all possible partitions                                                                                                              │
  │ 225 +     points = list(space.underlying_set)                                                                                                          │
  │ 226 +     n = len(points)                                                                                                                              │
  │ 227 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 228 +     # Generate all non-trivial partitions                                                                                                        │
  │ 229 +     from itertools import combinations                                                                                                           │
  │ 230 +     for r in range(1, n):                                                                                                                        │
  │ 231 +         for subset_tuple in combinations(points, r):                                                                                             │
  │ 232 +             U = set(subset_tuple)                                                                                                                │
  │ 233 +             V = space.underlying_set - U                                                                                                         │
  │ 234 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 235 +             U_frozen = frozenset(U)                                                                                                              │
  │ 236 +             V_frozen = frozenset(V)                                                                                                              │
  │ 237 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 238 +             # Check if both are open                                                                                                             │
  │ 239 +             if U_frozen in space.open_sets and V_frozen in space.open_sets:                                                                      │
  │ 240 +                 proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                             │
  │ 241 +                     conclusion=f"{space.name} is NOT connected",                                                                                 │
  │ 242 +                     premises=[f"X = U ∪ V where U={U}, V={V} are disjoint open sets"],                                                           │
  │ 243 +                     rule="connected_counterexample",                                                                                             │
  │ 244 +                     derivation=[]                                                                                                                │
  │ 245 +                 )                                                                                                                                │
  │ 246 +                 return False, proof                                                                                                              │
  │ 247 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 248 +     proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                         │
  │ 249 +         conclusion=f"{space.name} is connected",                                                                                                 │
  │ 250 +         premises=["no separation into disjoint open sets"],                                                                                      │
  │ 251 +         rule="connected_definition",                                                                                                             │
  │ 252 +         derivation=[]                                                                                                                            │
  │ 253 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 254 +     return True, proof                                                                                                                           │
  │ 255 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 256 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 257 + def continuous_map(f: Callable[[Point], Point],                                                                                                  │
  │ 258 +                    source: TopologicalSpace,                                                                                                     │
  │ 259 +                    target: TopologicalSpace) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                        │
  │ 260 +     """Verify f: source → target is continuous.                                                                                                  │
  │ 261 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 262 +     f is continuous if for every open V in target, f⁻¹(V) is open in source.                                                                     │
  │ 263 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 264 +     # Check preimage of every open set in target                                                                                                 │
  │ 265 +     for V in target.open_sets:                                                                                                                   │
  │ 266 +         preimage = {p for p in source.underlying_set if f(p) in V}                                                                               │
  │ 267 +         preimage_frozen = frozenset(preimage)                                                                                                    │
  │ 268 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 269 +         if preimage_frozen not in source.open_sets:                                                                                              │
  │ 270 +             proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                 │
  │ 271 +                 conclusion="f is NOT continuous",                                                                                                │
  │ 272 +                 premises=[f"preimage of {V} is {preimage}, not open in source"],                                                                 │
  │ 273 +                 rule="continuity_failure",                                                                                                       │
  │ 274 +                 derivation=[]                                                                                                                    │
  │ 275 +             )                                                                                                                                    │
  │ 276 +             return False, proof                                                                                                                  │
  │ 277 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 278 +     proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                         │
  │ 279 +         conclusion="f is continuous",                                                                                                            │
  │ 280 +         premises=["preimage of every open set is open"],                                                                                         │
  │ 281 +         rule="continuity_definition",                                                                                                            │
  │ 282 +         derivation=[]                                                                                                                            │
  │ 283 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 284 +     return True, proof                                                                                                                           │
  │ 285 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 286 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 287 + def homeomorphism_check(f: Callable[[Point], Point],                                                                                             │
  │ 288 +                         g: Callable[[Point], Point],                                                                                             │
  │ 289 +                         space1: TopologicalSpace,                                                                                                │
  │ 290 +                         space2: TopologicalSpace) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                   │
  │ 291 +     """Verify f: space1 → space2 is a homeomorphism with inverse g.                                                                              │
  │ 292 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 293 +     Conditions:                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 294 +     1. f is bijective                                                                                                                            │
  │ 295 +     2. f is continuous                                                                                                                           │
  │ 296 +     3. g = f⁻¹ is continuous                                                                                                                     │
  │ 297 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 298 +     # Check f is bijective                                                                                                                       │
  │ 299 +     images = {f(p) for p in space1.underlying_set}                                                                                               │
  │ 300 +     if images != space2.underlying_set:                                                                                                          │
  │ 301 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 302 +             conclusion="f is not surjective",                                                                                                    │
  │ 303 +             premises=[],                                                                                                                         │
  │ 304 +             rule="bijectivity_failure",                                                                                                          │
  │ 305 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 306 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 307 +         return False, proof                                                                                                                      │
  │ 308 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 309 +     if len(images) != len(space1.underlying_set):                                                                                                │
  │ 310 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 311 +             conclusion="f is not injective",                                                                                                     │
  │ 312 +             premises=[],                                                                                                                         │
  │ 313 +             rule="bijectivity_failure",                                                                                                          │
  │ 314 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 315 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 316 +         return False, proof                                                                                                                      │
  │ 317 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 318 +     # Check g is inverse                                                                                                                         │
  │ 319 +     for p in space1.underlying_set:                                                                                                              │
  │ 320 +         if g(f(p)) != p:                                                                                                                         │
  │ 321 +             proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                 │
  │ 322 +                 conclusion="g is not inverse of f",                                                                                              │
  │ 323 +                 premises=[f"g(f({p})) = {g(f(p))} ≠ {p}"],                                                                                       │
  │ 324 +                 rule="inverse_failure",                                                                                                          │
  │ 325 +                 derivation=[]                                                                                                                    │
  │ 326 +             )                                                                                                                                    │
  │ 327 +             return False, proof                                                                                                                  │
  │ 328 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 329 +     # Check continuity                                                                                                                           │
  │ 330 +     f_cont, f_proof = continuous_map(f, space1, space2)                                                                                          │
  │ 331 +     if not f_cont:                                                                                                                               │
  │ 332 +         return False, f_proof                                                                                                                    │
  │ 333 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 334 +     g_cont, g_proof = continuous_map(g, space2, space1)                                                                                          │
  │ 335 +     if not g_cont:                                                                                                                               │
  │ 336 +         return False, g_proof                                                                                                                    │
  │ 337 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 338 +     proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                         │
  │ 339 +         conclusion=f"{space1.name} ≅ {space2.name} (homeomorphic)",                                                                              │
  │ 340 +         premises=["f bijective", "f continuous", "f⁻¹ continuous"],                                                                              │
  │ 341 +         rule="homeomorphism_definition",                                                                                                         │
  │ 342 +         derivation=[f_proof, g_proof]                                                                                                            │
  │ 343 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 344 +     return True, proof                                                                                                                           │
  │ 345 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 346 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 347 + # Example: Discrete topology                                                                                                                     │
  │ 348 + def discrete_topology(points: Set[Point], name: str = "Discrete") -> TopologicalSpace:                                                           │
  │ 349 +     """Every subset is open."""                                                                                                                  │
  │ 350 +     from itertools import chain, combinations                                                                                                    │
  │ 351 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 352 +     # Power set                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 353 +     power_set = set(chain.from_iterable(                                                                                                         │
  │ 354 +         combinations(points, r) for r in range(len(points) + 1)                                                                                  │
  │ 355 +     ))                                                                                                                                           │
  │ 356 +     open_sets = {frozenset(s) for s in power_set}                                                                                                │
  │ 357 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 358 +     return TopologicalSpace(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 359 +         name=name,                                                                                                                               │
  │ 360 +         underlying_set=points,                                                                                                                   │
  │ 361 +         open_sets=open_sets                                                                                                                      │
  │ 362 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 363 +                                                                                                                                                  │
