  │  20 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  21 + @dataclass                                                                                                                                       │
  │  22 + class SigmaAlgebra:                                                                                                                              │
  │  23 +     """A sigma-algebra (σ-algebra) over a set X.                                                                                                 │
  │  24 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  25 +     Axioms:                                                                                                                                      │
  │  26 +     1. X ∈ Σ (contains whole space)                                                                                                              │
  │  27 +     2. Closed under complement: A ∈ Σ ⇒ X\A ∈ Σ                                                                                                  │
  │  28 +     3. Closed under countable unions: A₁, A₂, ... ∈ Σ ⇒ ∪Aᵢ ∈ Σ                                                                                  │
  │  29 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │  30 +     name: str                                                                                                                                    │
  │  31 +     whole_space: FrozenSet[str]                                                                                                                  │
  │  32 +     sets: Set[FrozenSet[str]]                                                                                                                    │
  │  33 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  34 +     def __post_init__(self):                                                                                                                     │
  │  35 +         """Validate sigma-algebra axioms."""                                                                                                     │
  │  36 +         # Axiom 1: Whole space is in the collection                                                                                              │
  │  37 +         assert self.whole_space in self.sets, "Whole space must be in sigma-algebra"                                                             │
  │  38 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  39 +         # Axiom 2: Closed under complement                                                                                                       │
  │  40 +         for s in self.sets:                                                                                                                      │
  │  41 +             complement = self.whole_space - s                                                                                                    │
  │  42 +             assert complement in self.sets, f"Complement of {s} not in sigma-algebra"                                                            │
  │  43 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  44 +         # Axiom 3: For finite case, check finite unions                                                                                          │
  │  45 +         # (Full countable closure requires infinite sets)                                                                                        │
  │  46 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  47 +     def check_complement_closed(self) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                               │
  │  48 +         """Verify closure under complement."""                                                                                                   │
  │  49 +         for s in self.sets:                                                                                                                      │
  │  50 +             complement = self.whole_space - s                                                                                                    │
  │  51 +             if complement not in self.sets:                                                                                                      │
  │  52 +                 return False, ProofObject(                                                                                                       │
  │  53 +                     conclusion=f"Not closed under complement",                                                                                   │
  │  54 +                     premises=[f"{s} ∈ Σ but complement {complement} ∉ Σ"],                                                                       │
  │  55 +                     rule="sigma_algebra_axiom_2",                                                                                                │
  │  56 +                     derivation=[]                                                                                                                │
  │  57 +                 )                                                                                                                                │
  │  58 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  59 +         return True, ProofObject(                                                                                                                │
  │  60 +             conclusion=f"{self.name} is closed under complement",                                                                                │
  │  61 +             premises=[f"verified for all {len(self.sets)} sets"],                                                                                │
  │  62 +             rule="sigma_algebra_verification",                                                                                                   │
  │  63 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │  64 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │  65 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  66 +     def check_union_closed(self) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                                    │
  │  67 +         """Verify closure under finite unions."""                                                                                                │
  │  68 +         sets_list = list(self.sets)                                                                                                              │
  │  69 +         for i, a in enumerate(sets_list):                                                                                                        │
  │  70 +             for b in sets_list[i:]:                                                                                                              │
  │  71 +                 union = a | b                                                                                                                    │
  │  72 +                 if union not in self.sets:                                                                                                       │
  │  73 +                     return False, ProofObject(                                                                                                   │
  │  74 +                         conclusion=f"Not closed under union",                                                                                    │
  │  75 +                         premises=[f"{a} ∪ {b} = {union} ∉ Σ"],                                                                                   │
  │  76 +                         rule="sigma_algebra_axiom_3",                                                                                            │
  │  77 +                         derivation=[]                                                                                                            │
  │  78 +                     )                                                                                                                            │
  │  79 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  80 +         return True, ProofObject(                                                                                                                │
  │  81 +             conclusion=f"{self.name} is closed under finite unions",                                                                             │
  │  82 +             premises=["verified for all pairs"],                                                                                                 │
  │  83 +             rule="sigma_algebra_verification",                                                                                                   │
  │  84 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │  85 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │  86 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  87 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  88 + @dataclass                                                                                                                                       │
  │  89 + class Measure:                                                                                                                                   │
  │  90 +     """A measure μ on a sigma-algebra (X, Σ).                                                                                                    │
  │  91 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  92 +     Axioms:                                                                                                                                      │
  │  93 +     1. Non-negativity: μ(A) ≥ 0 for all A ∈ Σ                                                                                                    │
  │  94 +     2. Null empty set: μ(∅) = 0                                                                                                                  │
  │  95 +     3. Countable additivity: for disjoint sets, μ(∪Aᵢ) = Σμ(Aᵢ)                                                                                  │
  │  96 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │  97 +     name: str                                                                                                                                    │
  │  98 +     sigma_algebra: SigmaAlgebra                                                                                                                  │
  │  99 +     values: Dict[FrozenSet[str], Fraction]                                                                                                       │
  │ 100 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 101 +     def __post_init__(self):                                                                                                                     │
  │ 102 +         """Validate measure axioms."""                                                                                                           │
  │ 103 +         # Axiom 2: μ(∅) = 0                                                                                                                      │
  │ 104 +         empty = frozenset()                                                                                                                      │
  │ 105 +         assert self.values.get(empty, Fraction(0)) == 0, "Measure of empty set must be 0"                                                        │
  │ 106 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 107 +         # Axiom 1: Non-negativity                                                                                                                │
  │ 108 +         for s, v in self.values.items():                                                                                                         │
  │ 109 +             assert v >= 0, f"Measure must be non-negative: μ({s}) = {v}"                                                                         │
  │ 110 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 111 +     def measure(self, s: FrozenSet[str]) -> Tuple[Fraction, ProofObject]:                                                                        │
  │ 112 +         """Get measure of a set."""                                                                                                              │
  │ 113 +         if s not in self.values:                                                                                                                 │
  │ 114 +             raise ValueError(f"Set {s} not in measure domain")                                                                                   │
  │ 115 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 116 +         value = self.values[s]                                                                                                                   │
  │ 117 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 118 +             conclusion=f"μ({s}) = {value}",                                                                                                      │
  │ 119 +             premises=["measure lookup"],                                                                                                         │
  │ 120 +             rule="measure_definition",                                                                                                           │
  │ 121 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 122 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 123 +         return value, proof                                                                                                                      │
  │ 124 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 125 +     def check_countable_additivity(self, sets: List[FrozenSet[str]]) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                │
  │ 126 +         """Verify μ(∪Aᵢ) = Σμ(Aᵢ) for disjoint sets."""                                                                                          │
  │ 127 +         # Check disjointness                                                                                                                     │
  │ 128 +         for i, a in enumerate(sets):                                                                                                             │
  │ 129 +             for b in sets[i+1:]:                                                                                                                 │
  │ 130 +                 if a & b:                                                                                                                        │
  │ 131 +                     return False, ProofObject(                                                                                                   │
  │ 132 +                         conclusion="Sets are not disjoint",                                                                                      │
  │ 133 +                         premises=[f"{a} ∩ {b} = {a & b} ≠ ∅"],                                                                                   │
  │ 134 +                         rule="disjointness_failure",                                                                                             │
  │ 135 +                         derivation=[]                                                                                                            │
  │ 136 +                     )                                                                                                                            │
  │ 137 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 138 +         # Calculate union measure                                                                                                                │
  │ 139 +         union = frozenset().union(*sets) if sets else frozenset()                                                                                │
  │ 140 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 141 +         if union not in self.values:                                                                                                             │
  │ 142 +             return False, ProofObject(                                                                                                           │
  │ 143 +                 conclusion="Union not in sigma-algebra",                                                                                         │
  │ 144 +                 premises=[],                                                                                                                     │
  │ 145 +                 rule="domain_failure",                                                                                                           │
  │ 146 +                 derivation=[]                                                                                                                    │
  │ 147 +             )                                                                                                                                    │
  │ 148 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 149 +         left = self.values[union]                                                                                                                │
  │ 150 +         right = sum(self.values.get(s, Fraction(0)) for s in sets)                                                                               │
  │ 151 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 152 +         if left != right:                                                                                                                        │
  │ 153 +             return False, ProofObject(                                                                                                           │
  │ 154 +                 conclusion="Countable additivity violated",                                                                                      │
  │ 155 +                 premises=[f"μ(∪Aᵢ) = {left}, Σμ(Aᵢ) = {right}"],                                                                                 │
  │ 156 +                 rule="additivity_failure",                                                                                                       │
  │ 157 +                 derivation=[]                                                                                                                    │
  │ 158 +             )                                                                                                                                    │
  │ 159 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 160 +         return True, ProofObject(                                                                                                                │
  │ 161 +             conclusion="Countable additivity verified",                                                                                          │
  │ 162 +             premises=[f"μ(∪Aᵢ) = {left} = Σμ(Aᵢ)"],                                                                                              │
  │ 163 +             rule="countable_additivity",                                                                                                         │
  │ 164 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 165 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 166 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 167 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 168 + @dataclass                                                                                                                                       │
  │ 169 + class ProbabilitySpace:                                                                                                                          │
  │ 170 +     """A probability space (Ω, F, P).                                                                                                            │
  │ 171 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 172 +     A measure space where P(Ω) = 1.                                                                                                              │
  │ 173 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 174 +     name: str                                                                                                                                    │
  │ 175 +     sample_space: SigmaAlgebra                                                                                                                   │
  │ 176 +     probability_measure: Measure                                                                                                                 │
  │ 177 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 178 +     def __post_init__(self):                                                                                                                     │
  │ 179 +         """Validate probability measure."""                                                                                                      │
  │ 180 +         # P(Ω) = 1                                                                                                                               │
  │ 181 +         assert self.probability_measure.values[self.sample_space.whole_space] == 1, \                                                            │
  │ 182 +             "Probability of sample space must be 1"                                                                                              │
  │ 183 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 184 +     def prob(self, event: FrozenSet[str]) -> Tuple[Fraction, ProofObject]:                                                                       │
  │ 185 +         """P(event) with proof."""                                                                                                               │
  │ 186 +         value, measure_proof = self.probability_measure.measure(event)                                                                           │
  │ 187 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 188 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 189 +             conclusion=f"P({event}) = {value}",                                                                                                  │
  │ 190 +             premises=[measure_proof.conclusion],                                                                                                 │
  │ 191 +             rule="probability_definition",                                                                                                       │
  │ 192 +             derivation=[measure_proof]                                                                                                           │
  │ 193 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 194 +         return value, proof                                                                                                                      │
  │ 195 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 196 +     def conditional_probability(self,                                                                                                            │
  │ 197 +                                  event_a: FrozenSet[str],                                                                                        │
  │ 198 +                                  event_b: FrozenSet[str]) -> Tuple[Fraction, ProofObject]:                                                       │
  │ 199 +         """P(A|B) = P(A ∩ B) / P(B), where P(B) > 0."""                                                                                          │
  │ 200 +         prob_b, _ = self.prob(event_b)                                                                                                           │
  │ 201 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 202 +         if prob_b == 0:                                                                                                                          │
  │ 203 +             raise ValueError("P(B) = 0, conditional probability undefined")                                                                      │
  │ 204 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 205 +         intersection = event_a & event_b                                                                                                         │
  │ 206 +         prob_intersection, _ = self.prob(intersection)                                                                                           │
  │ 207 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 208 +         result = prob_intersection / prob_b                                                                                                      │
  │ 209 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 210 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 211 +             conclusion=f"P({event_a}|{event_b}) = {result}",                                                                                     │
  │ 212 +             premises=[                                                                                                                           │
  │ 213 +                 f"P({event_a} ∩ {event_b}) = {prob_intersection}",                                                                               │
  │ 214 +                 f"P({event_b}) = {prob_b}"                                                                                                       │
  │ 215 +             ],                                                                                                                                   │
  │ 216 +             rule="conditional_probability",                                                                                                      │
  │ 217 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 218 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 219 +         return result, proof                                                                                                                     │
  │ 220 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 221 +     def check_independence(self,                                                                                                                 │
  │ 222 +                           event_a: FrozenSet[str],                                                                                               │
  │ 223 +                           event_b: FrozenSet[str]) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                  │
  │ 224 +         """Check if P(A ∩ B) = P(A) * P(B)."""                                                                                                   │
  │ 225 +         prob_a, _ = self.prob(event_a)                                                                                                           │
  │ 226 +         prob_b, _ = self.prob(event_b)                                                                                                           │
  │ 227 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 228 +         intersection = event_a & event_b                                                                                                         │
  │ 229 +         prob_intersection, _ = self.prob(intersection)                                                                                           │
  │ 230 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 231 +         expected = prob_a * prob_b                                                                                                               │
  │ 232 +         is_independent = (prob_intersection == expected)                                                                                         │
  │ 233 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 234 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 235 +             conclusion=f"{event_a} and {event_b} are {'independent' if is_independent else 'dependent'}",                                        │
  │ 236 +             premises=[                                                                                                                           │
  │ 237 +                 f"P(A ∩ B) = {prob_intersection}",                                                                                               │
  │ 238 +                 f"P(A) * P(B) = {expected}"                                                                                                      │
  │ 239 +             ],                                                                                                                                   │
  │ 240 +             rule="independence_definition",                                                                                                      │
  │ 241 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 242 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 243 +         return is_independent, proof                                                                                                             │
  │ 244 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 245 +     def total_probability(self,                                                                                                                  │
  │ 246 +                          partition: List[FrozenSet[str]],                                                                                        │
  │ 247 +                          event: FrozenSet[str]) -> Tuple[Fraction, ProofObject]:                                                                 │
  │ 248 +         """Law of total probability: P(A) = Σ P(A|Bᵢ) * P(Bᵢ)."""                                                                                │
  │ 249 +         # Verify partition                                                                                                                       │
  │ 250 +         union = frozenset().union(*partition) if partition else frozenset()                                                                      │
  │ 251 +         if union != self.sample_space.whole_space:                                                                                               │
  │ 252 +             raise ValueError("Partition does not cover sample space")                                                                            │
  │ 253 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 254 +         for i, a in enumerate(partition):                                                                                                        │
  │ 255 +             for b in partition[i+1:]:                                                                                                            │
  │ 256 +                 if a & b:                                                                                                                        │
  │ 257 +                     raise ValueError("Partition sets must be disjoint")                                                                          │
  │ 258 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 259 +         # Calculate                                                                                                                              │
  │ 260 +         total = Fraction(0)                                                                                                                      │
  │ 261 +         terms = []                                                                                                                               │
  │ 262 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 263 +         for b in partition:                                                                                                                      │
  │ 264 +             prob_b, _ = self.prob(b)                                                                                                             │
  │ 265 +             if prob_b > 0:                                                                                                                       │
  │ 266 +                 prob_a_given_b, _ = self.conditional_probability(event, b)                                                                       │
  │ 267 +                 term = prob_a_given_b * prob_b                                                                                                   │
  │ 268 +                 total += term                                                                                                                    │
  │ 269 +                 terms.append(f"P(A|{b})*P({b})={term}")                                                                                          │
  │ 270 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 271 +         # Verify matches P(A)                                                                                                                    │
  │ 272 +         prob_a, _ = self.prob(event)                                                                                                             │
  │ 273 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 274 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │
  │ 275 +             conclusion=f"P({event}) = {total} = {' + '.join(terms)}",                                                                            │
  │ 276 +             premises=["law of total probability"],                                                                                               │
  │ 277 +             rule="total_probability",                                                                                                            │
  │ 278 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │ 279 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 280 +         return total, proof                                                                                                                      │
  │ 281 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 282 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 283 + def bayes_theorem(prior: Fraction,                                                                                                               │
  │ 284 +                   likelihood: Fraction,                                                                                                          │
  │ 285 +                   evidence: Fraction) -> Tuple[Fraction, ProofObject]:                                                                           │
  │ 286 +     """Bayes' theorem: P(H|E) = P(E|H) * P(H) / P(E).                                                                                            │
  │ 287 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 288 +     Args:                                                                                                                                        │
  │ 289 +         prior: P(H) - prior probability of hypothesis                                                                                            │
  │ 290 +         likelihood: P(E|H) - probability of evidence given hypothesis                                                                            │
  │ 291 +         evidence: P(E) - total probability of evidence                                                                                           │
  │ 292 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 293 +     Returns:                                                                                                                                     │
  │ 294 +         posterior: P(H|E) - posterior probability                                                                                                │
  │ 295 +         proof: derivation proof                                                                                                                  │
  │ 296 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 297 +     if evidence == 0:                                                                                                                            │
  │ 298 +         raise ValueError("P(E) = 0, Bayes' theorem undefined")                                                                                   │
  │ 299 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 300 +     posterior = (likelihood * prior) / evidence                                                                                                  │
  │ 301 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 302 +     proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                         │
  │ 303 +         conclusion=f"P(H|E) = {posterior}",                                                                                                      │
  │ 304 +         premises=[                                                                                                                               │
  │ 305 +             f"P(H) = {prior}",                                                                                                                   │
  │ 306 +             f"P(E|H) = {likelihood}",                                                                                                            │
  │ 307 +             f"P(E) = {evidence}"                                                                                                                 │
  │ 308 +         ],                                                                                                                                       │
  │ 309 +         rule="bayes_theorem",                                                                                                                    │
  │ 310 +         derivation=[]                                                                                                                            │
  │ 311 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 312 +     return posterior, proof                                                                                                                      │
  │ 313 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 314 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 315 + # Entropy and Information Theory                                                                                                                 │
  │ 316 + def entropy(probabilities: List[Fraction]) -> Tuple[Fraction, ProofObject]:                                                                      │
  │ 317 +     """Shannon entropy: H(X) = -Σ pᵢ log₂(pᵢ).                                                                                                   │
  │ 318 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 319 +     Returns entropy in bits (using log2 approximation with Fraction).                                                                            │
  │ 320 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 321 +     import math                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 322 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 323 +     # Check probabilities sum to 1                                                                                                               │
  │ 324 +     if sum(probabilities) != 1:                                                                                                                  │
  │ 325 +         raise ValueError("Probabilities must sum to 1")                                                                                          │
  │ 326 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 327 +     # Calculate entropy                                                                                                                          │
  │ 328 +     h = Fraction(0)                                                                                                                              │
  │ 329 +     for p in probabilities:                                                                                                                      │
  │ 330 +         if p > 0:                                                                                                                                │
  │ 331 +             # Approximate -p * log2(p) using Fraction                                                                                            │
  │ 332 +             # For exact: would need symbolic log                                                                                                 │
  │ 333 +             log_p = Fraction(int(-math.log2(float(p)) * 1000000), 1000000)                                                                       │
  │ 334 +             h += p * log_p                                                                                                                       │
  │ 335 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 336 +     proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                         │
  │ 337 +         conclusion=f"H = {h} bits",                                                                                                              │
  │ 338 +         premises=[f"distribution: {probabilities}"],                                                                                             │
  │ 339 +         rule="entropy_definition",                                                                                                               │
  │ 340 +         derivation=[]                                                                                                                            │
  │ 341 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 342 +     return h, proof                                                                                                                              │
  │ 343 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 344 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 345 + def mutual_information(joint_probs: Dict[Tuple[str, str], Fraction],                                                                             │
  │ 346 +                        marginal_x: Dict[str, Fraction],                                                                                          │
  │ 347 +                        marginal_y: Dict[str, Fraction]) -> Tuple[Fraction, ProofObject]:                                                         │
  │ 348 +     """I(X;Y) = Σ P(x,y) log(P(x,y) / (P(x) * P(y))).                                                                                            │
  │ 349 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 350 +     Measures dependence between random variables.                                                                                                │
  │ 351 +     """                                                                                                                                          │
  │ 352 +     import math                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 353 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 354 +     mi = Fraction(0)                                                                                                                             │
  │ 355 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 356 +     for (x, y), p_xy in joint_probs.items():                                                                                                     │
  │ 357 +         if p_xy > 0:                                                                                                                             │
  │ 358 +             p_x = marginal_x[x]                                                                                                                  │
  │ 359 +             p_y = marginal_y[y]                                                                                                                  │
  │ 360 +             ratio = p_xy / (p_x * p_y)                                                                                                           │
  │ 361 +             log_ratio = Fraction(int(math.log2(float(ratio)) * 1000000), 1000000)                                                                │
  │ 362 +             mi += p_xy * log_ratio                                                                                                               │
  │ 363 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 364 +     proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                         │
  │ 365 +         conclusion=f"I(X;Y) = {mi} bits",                                                                                                        │
  │ 366 +         premises=["mutual information formula"],                                                                                                 │
  │ 367 +         rule="mutual_information",                                                                                                               │
  │ 368 +         derivation=[]                                                                                                                            │
  │ 369 +     )                                                                                                                                            │
  │ 370 +     return mi, proof                                                                                                                             │

  │  63 +                                                                                                                                                  │

  │  65 +         """Check if DFA accepts input string."""                                                                                                 │
  │  66 +         current = self.start_state                                                                                                               │
  │  67 +         trace = [current]                                                                                                                        │
  │  68 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  69 +         for symbol in input_string:                                                                                                              │
  │  70 +             next_state = self.delta(current, symbol)                                                                                             │
  │  71 +             if next_state is None:                                                                                                               │
  │  72 +                 proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                             │
  │  73 +                     conclusion=f"Rejected: no transition from {current} on {symbol}",                                                            │
  │  74 +                     premises=[f"input: {''.join(str(s) for s in input_string)}"],                                                                │
  │  75 +                     rule="dfa_rejection",                                                                                                        │
  │  76 +                     derivation=[]                                                                                                                │
  │  77 +                 )                                                                                                                                │
  │  78 +                 return False, proof                                                                                                              │
  │  79 +             current = next_state                                                                                                                 │
  │  80 +             trace.append(current)                                                                                                                │
  │  81 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  82 +         accepted = current in self.accept_states                                                                                                 │
  │  83 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  84 +         proof = ProofObject(                                                                                                                     │

  │  87 +             rule="dfa_acceptance",                                                                                                               │
  │  88 +             derivation=[]                                                                                                                        │
  │  89 +         )                                                                                                                                        │
  │  90 +         return accepted, proof                                                                                                                   │
  │  91 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │  92 +     def verify_deterministic(self) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                                                                                  │
  │  93 +         """Verify DFA is deterministic: exactly one transition per (state, symbol)."""                                                           │

  │ 103 +                     )                                                                                                                            │
  │ 104 +                                                                                                                                                  │
  │ 105 +         return True, ProofObject(                                                                                                                │
  │ 106 +             conclusion="DFA is deterministic",                                                                                                   │

  │ 116 +                                                                                                                                                  │
